Could derive via convolutions:
For positive k: c_k = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-k} h_i (1 - h_{i+k}). Gradient:
∂/∂h_i c_k:
- if i ≤ n-1-k (as first): 1 - h_{i+k}
- if i ≥ k (as second for term h_{i-k}(1-h_i)): -h_{i-k}
So for a given i, sum over k=1..n-1: (1-h_{i+k}) for i+k<n plus -h_{i-k} for i-k≥0. For negative shifts k=-d: c_{-d}=∑_{i=d}^{n-1} h_i (1-h_{i-d}). Gradient: first contributes 1-h_{i-d} if i≥d; second -h_{i+d} if i+d<n.
Actually this is same. Thus gradient of weighted sum W=∑_d w_d c_d + w_{-d} c_{-d}: Let pos weight P_d for c_d, neg weight N_d for c_{-d}. Contribution to grad[i] =
  for d=1..n-1:

Combine for d=1..n-1:

Left side (i-d>=0): from positive d as second: -W_d h_{i-d}; from negative -d as first: W_{-d}*(1-h_{i-d}) => W_{-d} - W_{-d}h - W_d h.
Thus G_i = ∑_{d: i+d<n} [ W_d*(1 - h_{i+d}) - W_{-d}*h_{i+d} ] + ∑_{d: i-d>=0} [ W_{-d}*(1 - h_{i-d}) - W_d*h_{i-d} ] + W_0*(1-2h_i).

def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    # If n large, binary search lambda for L2 projection
    lo=np.minimum(h.min(), -1.0)  # for h-lambda, lower? need lambda range: h - lam in [0,1] => lam in [h-1, h]

Simpler: After projection, use iterative proportional redistribution as previous for exact. The L2 projection may already exact enough. Compute c not sensitive to 1e-12. Use project_feasible robust:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=n/2.0
    h=h.copy(); h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # L2 projection bisection
    lo=float(h.min()-1.0); hi=float(h.max()+1.0)
    for _ in range(70): ...
    h=np.clip(h - (lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # fine adjust
    for _ in range(20):
      diff=target-h.sum()
      if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
      if diff>0:
         room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
         if tot<1e-13: break
         # add proportional to room but not exceed; if diff < tot, add diff/tot? This can exceed for small? Use min(diff/room max).
         inc=diff/tot
         h=np.minimum(h+inc*room,1.0)
      else:
         tot=h.sum()
         if tot<1e-13: break
         dec=-diff/tot
         h=np.maximum(h-inc? Wait dec positive) h=np.maximum(h - (dec)*h,0.0)
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
This final adjustment ensures sum. But adding proportional to room may after clip not exact; loop.

FFT gradient:
```
def fft_pad_len(n):
    M=2*n-1
    return 1 << (M-1).bit_length()

def c_counts(h,n): return np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')

def smooth_value_counts(cnt,n,alpha):
    c=cnt*(2.0/n)
    m=c.max()
    if alpha<=0: return m
    z=alpha*(c-m); z-=z.max(); return m + (z.max()+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-z.max()))))/alpha
```
But for objective with penalty, we need c smooth. Use counts each time. Could precompute dx.

Gradient:
```
def grad_smooth_counts(cnt,h,n,alpha):
    c=cnt*(2.0/n); m=c.max(); z=alpha*(c-m); z-=z.max(); w=np.exp(z); w/=w.sum(); W=w*(2.0/n)
    return grad_weights(W,h,n)

def grad_weights(W,h,n):
    M=2*n-1; ctr=n-1; L=ff_len
    S = W[ctr+1:ctr+n] + W[ctr-1::-1]
    K=np.zeros(L); K[ctr+1:ctr+n]=S; K[ctr-1::-1]=S; K[ctr]=0
    Hf=np.fft.rfft(h,n=L); Kf=np.fft.rfft(K,n=L)
    T=np.fft.irfft(Hf*Kf,n=L)[ctr:ctr+n]
    pref_pos=np.zeros(n); pos=W[ctr+1:ctr+n]; pref_pos[1:]=np.cumsum(pos); pos_right=pref_pos[n-1::-1]
    neg=W[ctr-1::-1]; pref_neg=np.zeros(n); pref_neg[1:]=np.cumsum(neg)
    G=pos_right + pref_neg + W[ctr] - T - 2.0*W[ctr]*h
    return G
```
Check shapes: pref_neg length n, pref_neg[0]=0, i uses pref_neg[i]. Good. pos_right length n. Need if n=1? not.
Potential issue: K[ctr-1::-1]=S assumes ctr=n-1; K length L>=2n-1, indices 0..n-2 set; right set n..2n-2? center+1 to center+n-1. Good.

Objective with penalty:
```
def obj_pen(x,n,alpha,mu):
    cnt=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')
    val=smooth_value_counts(cnt,n,alpha)
    s=x.sum()-n/2
    return val+0.5*mu*s*s
```
Gradient:
```
def grad_pen(x,n,alpha,mu):
    cnt=np.correlate(x,1-x,'-full')
    g=grad_smooth_counts(cnt,x,n,alpha)
    g += mu*(x.sum()-n/2)
    return g
```
Wait derivative 0.5 μ s^2 -> μ s * ones.

def coord_feasible_point(y, A_pos, B_pos, A_neg, B_neg, B_zero, base0, s, x):
    if B_zero.size and np.max(B_zero) > y + 1e-10: return -1.0
    L=0.0; R=1.0
    if A_pos.size:
        ub=(y-B_pos)/A_pos

    if A_neg.size:
        lb=(y-B_neg)/A_neg
        L=max(L, float(lb.max()))
    if L>R: return -1

L-BFGS runner:
```
def lbfgs_runner(h,n,deadline,configs):
    best_h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=c5(project...)
    b=make_bounds(n)
    for alpha,mu,mx in configs:
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if rem<20: break
       options={'maxiter':min(int(rem*3), mx), 'ftol':1e-18,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':40}
       try: res=minimize(obj_pen,best_h,grad_pen,args=(n,alpha,mu),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=b,options=options)
       except: continue
       h2=project_feasible(res.x,n); c2=c5(h2)
       if c2<best_c: update
    return best_h,best_c
```

Pair polish algorithm detail:
```
def pair_polish(h,n,deadline,radius=25,deltas=None):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    cnt=c_counts(h,n); best_cnt=cnt.max()
    if deltas is None: deltas=(2e-4,1e-4,5e-5,2e-5,1e-5)
    acc_since_recomp=0
    for d in deltas:
        improved=True
        while improved and time.time()<deadline:
            improved=False; passes=0
            for i in range(n):
                if time.time()>deadline: return h, best_cnt*(2/n)
                jlo=max(0,i-radius); jhi=min(n,i+radius+1)
                for j in range(jlo,jhi):
                   if i==j: continue
                   # forward
                   if h[i] < 1.0 and h[j] > 0.0:
                       dm = min(d, 1.0 - h[i] + 1e-15, h[j] - 1e-15)
                       if dm > 1e-15:
                           apply_var_delta(i, dm, h,cnt,n)
                           m=cnt.max()
                           if m < best_cnt - 1e-13:
                               best_cnt=m; improved=True; acc+=1
                           else:
                               apply_var_delta(j, dm, h,cnt,n); apply_var_delta(i, -dm, h,cnt,n)
                   # backward
                   if h[i] >0 and h[j]<1:
                       dm=...
                   if acc%5000==0: recompute? can't inside without disturbing; maybe after i loop.
            if acc_since_recomp>10000:
                cnt=c_counts(h,n); best_cnt=min(best_cnt,cnt.max())? But current max should be <= best. Recompute current max and set best_cnt=cnt.max() because accepted only decreases. However if recompute errors, set current.
                acc=0
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    cnt=c_counts(h,n)
    return h, count*2/n
```
But if we recompute after accepted moves, best_cnt should become current max (which may be slightly different). Fine.

Detailed functions:
```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n: h = h[:n]; ...
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    lo = float(h.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(h.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=np.clip(h-mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    h=np.clip(h-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    # fine adjust
    for _ in range(30):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff) < 1e-12: break
       if diff >0:
          room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot < 1e-13: break
          h += (diff/tot)*room
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<1e-13: break
          h += (diff/tot)*h
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # one more correction to exact as much as possible
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       free=np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0-1e-12))[0]
       if free.size:
          h[free] += diff/free.size
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       elif diff>0 and np.any(h<1):
          idx=np.where(h<1)[0][:diff] # if diff small maybe one
          h[idx] += 1e-12  # not exact but ignore
    return h
```
The final adjustment with free equal could push some >1 if diff too large. But after L2 projection diff is tiny. Use safe:
```
if free.size:
   step=diff/free.size
   h[free]+ = np.clip(step, -1e-12? no). Actually if diff >0, room total >= diff; step <= max room? If all free values equal, step may exceed some room? L2 projection should distribute roughly; but to be safe if abs(step)>1? Use iterative; diff tiny.
```
Could just after projection and final diff accept 1e-12; evaluation integral tolerance likely fine but spec sum exactly? It says equivalently sum == n/2 exactly? In floating cannot exact. They likely check tolerance. But to be safe, do previous iterative adjust that converges. It changes only small. Good.

`c5_from_h`:
```
def c5_from_h(h,n): return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'))*(2.0/n))
```

`smooth_max`:
```
def smooth_max(cnt,n,alpha):
    c=cnt*(2.0/n)
    m=float(c.max())
    if alpha <= 1e-8 or n==0: return m
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    z-=z.max()
    return float(m + (z.max() + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-z.max()))))/alpha)
```
Potential log(0) if sum underflows? z max 0, exp(0)=1, sum≥1. Good.

`grad_weights` as above. Need `fft_len` compute each call? We can define:
```
def fft_len_for(n):
   m=2*n-1
   return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```
`(m-1).bit_length()` for m=1? n=1 not.
Potential numpy FFT length: if m=999, (998).bit_length()=10 ->1024. For m=997? okay. If m=2 (n=1.5) no.

`weights_for_smooth`:
```
def smooth_weights(cnt,n,alpha):
    c=cnt*(2/n); m=c.max(); z=alpha*(c-m); z-=z.max(); w=np.exp(z); w/=w.sum(); return w*(2/n)
```
Could compute c again in grad? Use same.

`grad_smooth(cnt,h,n,alpha)`:
```
W=smooth_weights(cnt,n,alpha); return grad_weights(W,h,n)
```

`obj_pen`, `grad_pen`:
```
def obj_pen(x,n,alpha,mu):
   cnt=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')
   val=smooth_max(cnt,n,alpha)
   s=float(np.sum(x)-0.5*n)
   return val + 0.5*mu*s*s

def grad_pen(x,n,alpha,mu):
   cnt=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')
   g=grad_smooth(cnt,x,n,alpha)
   s=float(np.sum(x)-0.5*n)
   g += mu*s
   return g
```
Check mu sign: derivative of 0.5 μ s^2 -> μ*s*1. Yes.

`obj_slq`, `grad_slq` no mu.

`lbfgs_opt`:
```
def lbfgs_opt(h,n,deadline,configs):
    best=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=c5_from_h(best,n)
    b=make_bounds(n)
    for alpha,mu,mx in configs:
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if rem < 15: break
       # limit maxiter by time estimate: max(20, min(mx, int(rem*10)))? 10 iter/s conservative.
       iters = int(min(mx, max(20, rem*6))) # 6 iter/s maybe
       try:
           res=minimize(obj_pen,best,grad_pen,args=(n,alpha,mu),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=b,options={'maxiter':iters,'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':50,'disp':False})
       except Exception:
           continue
       h2=project_feasible(res.x,n); c2=c5_from_h(h2,n)
       if c2 < best_c - 1e-16:
           best_c=c2; best=h2
    return best,best_c
```
But `res.x` may not exist if failed. Check `hasattr(res,'x')`. If not continue. If `res.fun` not better? We evaluate.
Need `make_bounds` top-level.

`slsqp_opt`:

def slsqp_opt(h,n,deadline,alpha,maxiter):
   rem=deadline-time.time()
   if rem < 30: return h,c
   if n > 900 and rem < 120: return h,c
   iters=int(min(maxiter, max(50, rem//3))) # maybe 1/3 sec per iter? safe 100/s?
   # SLSQP maybe 1-2s per 100, so rem//5.
   iters=int(min(maxiter, max(20, rem*0.8)))? Too high if slow. Use 300 for 500.
   if rem < 60: return
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':slq_eq,'jac':slq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
   try: res=minimize(obj_slq,h,grad_slq,args=(n,alpha),method='SLSQP',bounds=make_bounds(n),constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':iters,'ftol':1e-15,'disp':False})
   except: return
   h2=project_feasible(res.x,n); c2=...
   if c2<best: return
   return h,best
```

def upscale(h,nold,nnew):
    xold=np.linspace(0,2,nold,endpoint=False); xnew=np.linspace(0,2,nnew,endpoint=False)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(xnew,xold,h),nnew)
```

Let's carefully write pair_polish to be robust and not too slow. We need top-level `apply_var_delta`. It modifies h and cnt arrays in place. It should handle `n` maybe 500. For q choose.
```
def apply_var_delta(q, delta, h, cnt, n):
    ctr = n - 1
    x = h[q]
    nr = n - 1 - q
    if nr > 0:
        rr = h[q+1:q+n]
        cnt[ctr+1:ctr+1+nr] += delta * (1.0 - rr)
        cnt[ctr-nr:ctr] += -delta * rr[::-1]
    ql = q
    if ql > 0:
        ll = h[q-ql:q]
        cnt[ctr-ql:ctr] += delta * (1.0 - 2.0*ll)
    if delta != 0.0:
        cnt[ctr] += delta * (1.0 - 2.0*x) - delta*delta
    h[q] = x + delta
```
Wait for right second and left combined, for indices where both left and right exist, there are two additions to same negative slice: right second `cnt[ctr-nr:ctr] += -delta*rr[::-1]`, left combined `cnt[ctr-ql:ctr] += delta*(1-2*ll)`. Overlap okay.
But for right first, positive slice. For left positive? The left combined includes left neighbors as first in negative and second in positive, both at negative index. It does not add to positive index for left neighbor. But variable q as second in positive shift with left neighbor (q-d) has index positive? Wait revisit: Pair q and q-d (left). Unordered pair (q-d, q) with positive shift d: term h_{q-d}(1-h_q) contributes to positive shift c_{+d} (index center+d). q appears as second, derivative -h_{q-d} at center+d. Did we miss positive index for left neighbors! Earlier I combined left contributions at negative index mistakenly? Let's re-derive carefully.
For variable q, each other at position j = q-d (left) and j=q+d (right).
For unordered pair left j = q-d:
- Positive shift k = d: term h_j (1 - h_q) at index center+d. Derivative wrt h_q = -h_j.
- Negative shift k = -d: term h_q (1 - h_j) at index center-d. Derivative = (1 - h_j).
Total for left neighbor = delta * [ -h_j at pos index center+d, (1-h_j) at neg index center-d ].
For right neighbor j = q+d:
- Positive shift k=d: term h_q(1-h_j) at center+d derivative (1-h_j).
- Negative shift k=-d: term h_j(1-h_q) at center-d derivative -h_j.
Thus update formulas:
For right rr d=1..nr:
  cnt[ctr+d] += delta*(1-rr[d])
  cnt[ctr-d] += delta*(-rr[d])
For left ll d=1..ql:
  cnt[ctr+d] += delta*(-ll[d])
  cnt[ctr-d] += delta*(1-ll[d])
Not combined at same index! I earlier left combined to center-d only and missed positive index for left. That's a major bug. Need correct.
Let's test with q=0 (ql=0) okay. q=1 with left j=0: variable q h1. c_pos d1 includes h0(1-h1) term, derivative -h0 at index center+1. c_neg d1 includes h1(1-h0), derivative 1-h0 at index center-1. Need both.
Our earlier formula added only negative for left, wrong.
Correct apply_var_delta:
```
if nr>0:
    rr = h[q+1:q+n]  # d=1..nr
    cnt[ctr+1:ctr+1+nr] += delta*(1.0-rr)
    cnt[ctr-nr:ctr] += -delta*rr[::-1]
if ql>0:
    ll_rev = h[q-1::-1]  # d=1..ql (h[q-1], h[q-2],...)
    # positive index center+d d=1..ql: add delta*(-ll_rev)
    cnt[ctr+1:ctr+1+ql] += -delta*ll_rev
    # negative index center-d d=1..ql: slice ctr-ql:ctr increasing d=ql..1; values ll_rev reversed = h[q-ql]..h[q-1]
    cnt[ctr-ql:ctr] += delta*(1.0 - ll_rev[::-1])
```
Alternatively use ll=h[q-ql:q] (d=ql..1):
 positive: cnt[ctr+1:ctr+1+ql] += -delta*ll[::-1]
 negative: cnt[ctr-ql:ctr] += delta*(1.0 - ll)
Let's check ql=2: h[q-2]=d2, h[q-1]=d1. ll=[d2,d1]. positive index d1,d2 order => add -delta*[d1,d2] = -delta*ll[::-1]. negative index slice [ctr-2,ctr-1] order d2,d1 => add delta*(1-[d2,d1]) = delta*(1-ll). Good.
For right, rr=[d1,d2]. positive index d1,d2 -> delta*(1-rr). negative slice order d2,d1 -> -delta*rr[::-1]. Good.
Now test sequential swap a<b earlier with corrected formula:

- If q = s-d (second) and s = q+d, i.e., q+d < n: term h_{q+d}(1-h_q) at index center+d. Coeff -h_{q+d} (right neighbor).
Thus for index center+d (positive side), contributions: from left neighbor (1-h_left) and right neighbor -h_right.

- if q=s first, q+d<n: coeff (1-h_right) at index center-d.
- if q=s-d second, q-d>=0: coeff -h_left at index center-d.
So update formulas for variable q:

  pos side center+d: += delta * [ (left? 1-h_{q-d} if q-d>=0) + (right? -h_{q+d} if q+d<n) ]
  neg side center-d: += delta * [ (right? 1-h_{q+d}) + (left? -h_{q+d? wait left? -h_{q-d}) ]
Specifically:
For right neighbor j=q+d:
  at center+d (first? q=d? no, q as second) coeff -h_j.
  at center-d (q as first) coeff (1-h_j).
For left neighbor j=q-d:
  at center+d (q as first) coeff (1-h_j).
  at center-d (q as second) coeff -h_j.
Thus the signs are swapped compared to previous pair apply! In previous, right first was at center+d; correct is at center-d. Does this affect max search? Let's correct apply_var_delta for np.correlate mapping empirically with mapping center+d = ∑_s h_s (1-h_{s-d}). We'll implement using this.
```
def apply_var_delta(q, delta, h, cnt, n):
  ctr=n-1; x=h[q]
  nr=n-1-q
  if nr>0:
     rr=h[q+1:q+n]  # d=1..nr right
     # right neighbor at center+d: coeff -rr -> negative? index center+d slice increasing d
     cnt[ctr+1:ctr+1+nr] += -delta * rr
     # right neighbor at center-d: coeff 1-rr, slice center-nr:center order d=nr..1
     cnt[ctr-nr:ctr] += delta * (1.0 - rr[::-1])
  ql=q
  if ql>0:
     ll=h[q-ql:q]  # d=ql..1 (left oldest to nearest)
     # left neighbor at center+d: coeff 1-rr, need d=1..ql -> ll[::-1]
     cnt[ctr+1:ctr+1+ql] += delta * (1.0 - ll[::-1])
     # left neighbor at center-d: coeff -ll, slice d=ql..1 -> ll
     cnt[ctr-ql:ctr] += -delta * ll
  cnt[ctr] += delta*(1-2*x)-delta*delta
  h[q]=x+delta
```

Let's derive gradient for weighted sum over cnt[t] with weights W_t. For t>=center: d=t-center >=0, cnt[d]=∑_s h_s (1-h_{s-d}). Gradient wrt h_i:

- as second: if i+d<n: -h_{i+d} for d (right)
For t<center: d=center-t>0, cnt=-d = ∑_s h_s (1-h_{s+d}). Gradient:
- if i+d<n: 1-h_{i+d} (right)
- if i-d>=0: -h_{i+d? no -h_{i-d}} (left)
So general for distance d:
  contribution from weight at center+d (call Wplus[d]) to G_i:
     if left exists: Wplus[d]*(1-h_{i-d})
     if right exists: Wplus[d]*(-h_{i+d})
  contribution from weight at center-d (Wminus[d]):
     if right exists: Wminus[d]*(1-h_{i+d})
     if left exists: Wminus[d]*(-h_{i-d})
This is exactly the same as our previous formula if we rename W_d = Wminus? In previous formula: right contribution W_d*(1-h_right) - W_-d*h_right; left W_-d*(1-h_left) - W_d*h_left. Compare here: For right h_{i+d}: coefficient from Wplus[d] is -h; from Wminus[d] is (1-h). Total = Wminus[d] - (Wplus[d]+Wminus[d])h. For left h_{i-d}: coefficient Wplus[d]*(1-h)+Wminus[d]*(-h)=Wplus[d] - (Wplus+Wminus)h. Constants: for right: Wminus[d]; for left: Wplus[d].
Our earlier T formula used positive side W[center+d] as W_d and contribution constants pos_right = ∑ W_d for right, neg_left = ∑ W_-d for left. That is swapped: correct right constant should Wminus (center-d), left constant Wplus (center+d). But T (h coefficients) is symmetric sum (Wplus+Wminus), and constants are pos/neg swapped. Since the objective max array is not symmetric in weights (weights differ for + and - indices), using swapped constants gives wrong gradient unless Wplus=Wminus. Need fix grad_weights.

Correct grad_weights with np mapping:
Let WP[d] = W[center+d] for d=1..n-1 (weights at positive index, corresponds shift -d)
Let WM[d] = W[center-d] (weights at negative index, corresponds shift +d)
For each i:
  Right neighbors (i+d<n): constant WM[d]; h coefficient -(WP[d]+WM[d]).
  Left neighbors (i-d>=0): constant WP[d]; h coefficient -(WP[d]+WM[d]).
  Center diag: W[center]*(1-2h_i).
Thus constants:
  right_const_i = ∑_{d=1}^{n-1-i} WM[d]
  left_const_i = ∑_{d=1}^{i} WP[d]
T_i=∑_{d} S_d (h_right + h_left), S=WP+WM.
G = right_const + left_const + W0 - T - 2W0 h.
This is same as before but swap pos_right/pref_neg. Previously right_const used W_positive (center+d) and left_const W_negative (center-d). Need swap.

Wplus = W[ctr+1:ctr+n]  # d=1..n-1 (center+d)

```

```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-10
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 try: h0=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n=len(h0)
 except: h0=golden_start(500); n=500
 best_h=project_feasible(h0,n); best_c=c5_from_h(best_h,n); best_n=n
 if time.time()<deadline:
    end=min(deadline-500, time.time()+400)
    best_h,best_c=lbfgs_opt(best_h,best_n,end, MAIN_CONFIGS)
 if time.time()<deadline-100:
    best_h,best_c=pair_polish(best_h,best_n,min(deadline-300,time.time()+80), radius=15)
 if time.time()<deadline-150:
    end=min(deadline-120, time.time()+120)
    h,c=slsqp_opt(best_h,best_n,end,200000,500)
    if c<best_c: update
 if time.time()<deadline-90:
    best_h,best_c=pair_polish(best_h,best_n,min(deadline-90,time.time()+50),radius=12)
 if time.time()<deadline-80:
    end=min(deadline-70,time.time()+120)
    h,c=slsqp_opt(best_h,best_n,end,2000000,700)
    if c<best_c: update
 if time.time()<deadline-40:
    best_h,best_c=pair_polish(best_h,best_n,min(deadline-35,time.time()+50),radius=10)
 # restarts if time
 if time.time()<deadline-120:
    # perturbation
    hp=project_feasible(best_h+rng.normal(0,0.08,best_n),best_n)
    h,c=lbfgs_opt(hp,best_n,min(deadline-60,time.time()+80),RESTART_CONFIGS)
    if c<best_c: update
 if time.time()<deadline-100:
    hb=threshold_binary(best_h,best_n)
    h,c=lbfgs_opt(hb,best_n,min(deadline-50,time.time()+60),RESTART_CONFIGS)
    if c<best_c: update
 # high-res if time and best_n<1000
 if time.time()<deadline-260 and best_n<1000:
    n2=min(1000, 2*best_n)
    h2=upscale_h(best_h,best_n,n2)
    end=min(deadline-60, time.time()+200)
    h2,c2=lbfgs_opt(h2,n2,end,HIGHRES_CONFIGS)
    if c2<best_c and time<deadline-60:
       end2=min(deadline-40,time.time()+80); h2,c2=slsqp_opt(h2,n2,end2,1000000,400)
    if c2<best_c:
       best_h,best_n=h2,n2; best_c=c2
 # final quick pair
 if time.time()<deadline-12:
    best_h,best_c=pair_polish(best_h,best_n,deadline-6,radius=8)
 best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
 best_c=c5_from_h(best_h,best_n)
 return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

def pair_polish(h,n,deadline,radius=15):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy()
    cnt=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    best_cnt=float(cnt.max())
    acc=0
    for d in (5e-4,2e-4,1e-4,5e-5,2e-5,1e-5):
       for _ in range(3):
          if time.time()>=deadline: break
          improved=False
          for i in range(n):
             if time.time()>=deadline: break
             jlo=max(0,i-radius); jhi=min(n,i+radius+1)
             for j in range(jlo,jhi):
                if i==j: continue
                # move to i
                if h[i] < 1.0 and h[j] > 0.0:
                   dm = min(d, 1.0 - h[i], h[j])
                   if dm > 1e-14:
                      apply_var_delta(i,dm,h,cnt,n)
                      m=float(cnt.max())
                      if m < best_cnt - 1e-12:
                         best_cnt=m; improved=True; acc+=1
                         if acc % 5000 ==0:
                            # recompute to reduce drift, but h/cnt currently consistent; just ensure best_h
                            cnt=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
                            m2=float(cnt.max())
                            if m2 < best_cnt:
                               best_cnt=m2; best_h=h.copy()
                            else:
                               # keep state as is; best_cnt maybe best_h lower
                               # But current h accepted only if improved; if recompute m2 > best_h due drift/errors? unlikely. To be safe reset to best_h if m2 > best_cnt + 1e-9?
                               pass
                      else:
                         apply_var_delta(j,dm,h,cnt,n)
                         apply_var_delta(i,-dm,h,cnt,n)
                # move to j
                if h[i] > 0.0 and h[j] < 1.0:
                   dm=intern? Use min(d,h[i],1-h[j]) # Note after first move h changed; if first move was revert, restored; if accepted, h changed and second move may also. That's okay; sequence order arbitrary.

Implementation of project_feasible accurate:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if h.size != n:
       if h.size > n: h = h[:n]
       else: pad
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    lo=float(h.min())-1.0; hi=float(h.max())+1.0
    for _ in range(80): ...
    h=np.clip(h-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    for _ in range(50):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
       if diff >0:
          room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot < 1e-13: break
          h = h + (diff/tot)*room
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<1e-13: break
          h = h + (diff/tot)*h
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final tiny correction
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       free=np.where((h>1e-12)&(h<1.0-1e-12))[0]
       if free.size:
          h[free] += diff/free.size
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       else:
          # no free, do not risk
    return h
```
If `n` maybe odd, target .5; L2 projection gives fractional. final diff tiny.
If `h` all zeros and target >0: lo=-1 hi=1; bisection works. Fine.

def c5_from_h(h,n):
    if n<=0: return 0.0
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full'))*(2.0/float(n)))
```

`fft_len_for`:
```
def fft_len_for(n):
    m=2*n-1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```
For m=1 -> (0).bit_length()=0 ->1. okay.

`grad_weights` code:
```
def grad_weights(W, h, n):
    if n <= 1: return np.zeros(n)
    ctr = n - 1
    Wplus = W[ctr+1:ctr+n]
    Wminus = W[ctr-1::-1]
    S = Wplus + Wminus
    L = fft_len_for(n)
    K = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    K[ctr+1:ctr+n] = S
    K[ctr-1::-1] = S
    hf = np.fft.rfft(h, n=L)
    kf = np.fft.rfft(K, n=L)
    T = np.fft.irfft(hf * kf, n=L)[ctr:ctr+n]
    pref_plus = np.zeros(n)
    if S.size:
       pref_plus[1:] = np.cumsum(Wplus)
       pref_minus[1:] = np.cumsum(Wminus)
    right_const = pref_minus[n-1::-1]
    left_const = pref_plus
    G = right_const + left_const + W[ctr] - T - 2.0*W[ctr]*h
    return G
```
Need define pref_minus before. If n=2, S length1. pref arrays length2. K[ctr-1::-1] length? ctr=1, K[0::-1]? `K[ctr-1::-1]` means start 0 step -1 -> only index0, length1, good. `K[ctr+1:ctr+n]` start2 stop2? n=2, ctr+n=3, index 2..2? S length1, K[2:3]. But L for n=2: m=3, (2).bit_length()=2 ->4. K index3 unused. Good. Does circular conv with L=4 and center=1, positive side set index2 (d=1), negative index0 (d=1). Works. `T=irfft(...)[1:3]` length2. Good.

`smooth_weights`:
```
def smooth_weights(cnt,n,alpha):
    dx=2.0/n
    c=cnt*dx
    m=c.max()
    if alpha <= 0:
       w=np.full_like(c,1.0/len(c)); return w*dx
    z=alpha*(c-m); z-=z.max(); w=np.exp(z); w/=w.sum(); return w*dx
```

```
import numpy as np, time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

MAIN=((100.0,1.0,250),(1000.0,5.0,250),(10000.0,50.0,300),(100000.0,200.0,350),(1000000.0,2000.0,500))
REST=((10000.0,50.0,200),(500000.0,2000.0,300))
```

`project_feasible`:
```
def project(h,n):
 h=np.asarray(h,float).reshape(-1)
 if h.size<n:
   h=np.pad(h,(0,n-h.size))
 elif h.size>n: h=h[:n]
 h=np.clip(h,0,1); t=.5*n
 lo=float(h.min())-1; hi=float(h.max())+1
 for _ in range(70):
  m=.5*(lo+hi); s=np.clip(h-m,0,1).sum()
  if s>t: lo=m
  else: hi=m
 h=np.clip(h-.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
 for _ in range(30):
  d=t-h.sum()
  if abs(d)<1e-13: break
  if d>0:
   r=1-h; s=r.sum()
   if s<1e-13: break
   h=np.clip(h+(d/s)*r,0,1)
  else:
   s=h.sum()
   if s<1e-13: break
   h=np.clip(h+(d/s)*h,0,1)
 d=t-h.sum()
 if abs(d)>1e-15:
  f=np.where((h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12))[0]
  if f.size:
   h[f]+=d/f.size; h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 return h
```
Use name project not conflict? okay.

`c5`:
```
def c5(h,n): return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'))*2.0/n)
```

`fft_len`:
```
def fend(n):
 m=2*n-1; return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`smooth_max`:
```
def smax(cnt,n,alpha):
 c=cnt*2.0/n; m=c.max()
 if alpha<=0: return float(m)
 z=alpha*(c-m); z-=z.max()
 return float(m+(z.max()+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-z.max()))))/alpha)
```

`sw` weights:
```
def sw(cnt,n,alpha):
 c=cnt*2.0/n; m=c.max()
 if alpha<=0: return None
 z=alpha*(c-m); z-=z.max(); w=np.exp(z); w/=w.sum(); return w*2.0/n
```

`gradw`:
```
def gradw(W,h,n):
 if n<=1: return np.zeros(n)
 c=n-1; wp=W[c+1:c+n]; wm=W[c-1::-1]; s=wp+wm
 L=fend(n); K=np.zeros(L); K[c+1:c+n]=s; K[c-1::-1]=s
 T=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(h,n=L)*np.fft.rfft(K,n=L),n=L)[c:c+n]
 pp=np.zeros(n); pm=np.zeros(n)
 if s.size:
  pp[1:]=np.cumsum(wp); pm[1:]=np.cumsum(wm)
 G=pm[n-1::-1]+pp+W[c]-T-2*W[c]*h
 return G
```
Need if n=2 `c-1::-1` okay. If wp length0? n=1 handled.

`grads`:
```
def grads(cnt,h,n,alpha): W=sw(cnt,n,alpha); return gradw(W,h,n)
```

`objp`:
```
def objp(x,n,alpha,mu):
 cnt=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full'); v=smax(cnt,n,alpha); s=x.sum()-n/2.0; return v+.5*mu*s*s
```
`gradp`:
```
def gradp(x,n,alpha,mu):
 cnt=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full'); g=gradw(sw(cnt,n,alpha),x,n); s=x.sum()-n/2.0; g+=mu*s; return g
```
`objs`, `grads` (greply). Name conflict with grads? Use gsmooth.
```
def objs(x,n,alpha): return smax(np.correlate(x,1-x,'full'),n,alpha)
def gsmooth(x,n,alpha): return gradw(sw(np.correlate(x,1-x,'full'),n,alpha),x,n)
def slqeq(x,n): return x.sum()-n/2.0
def slqjac(x,n): return np.ones_like(x)
```

`lbfgs`:
```
def lbfgs(h,n,dl,configs):
 best=project(h,n); bc=c5(best,n)
 b=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
 for alpha,mu,mx in configs:
  rem=dl-time.time()
  if rem<12: break
  it=int(min(mx, max(10, int(rem*6))))
  try:
   r=minimize(objp,best,gradp,args=(n,alpha,mu),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=b,options={'maxiter':it,'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':50,'maxcor':50,'disp':False})
   if hasattr(r,'x'):
    h2=project(r.x,n); c2=c5(h2,n)
    if c2<bc: bc=c2; best=h2.copy()
  except Exception: pass
 return best,bc
```
Could `gradp` call `sw` with alpha; if alpha large, ok. `maxcor` keyword valid for L-BFGS-B? Yes.

`slq`:
```
def slq(h,n,dl,alpha,mx):
 h=project(h,n); bc=c5(h,n)
 rem=dl-time.time()
 if rem<25: return h,bc
 it=int(min(mx, max(20, int(rem*3))))
 cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':slqeq,'jac':slqjac,'args':(n,)}]
 try:
  r=minimize(objs,h,gsmooth,args=(n,alpha),method='SLSQP',bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':it,'ftol':1e-15,'disp':False})
  if hasattr(r,'x'):
   h2=project(r.x,n); c2=c5(h2,n)
   if c2<bc: return h2.copy(),c2
 except Exception: pass
 return h,bc
```
If rem*3 for 100s=300, okay. For 120s=360. Good.

`apply`:
```
def apply(q,d,h,cnt,n):
 c=n-1; x=h[q]; nr=n-1-q
 if nr>0:
  rr=h[q+1:q+n]
  cnt[c+1:c+1+nr]-=d*rr
  cnt[c-nr:c]+=d*(1.0-rr[::-1])
 ql=q
 if ql>0:
  ll=h[q-ql:q]
  cnt[c+1:c+1+ql]+=d*(1.0-ll[::-1])
  cnt[c-ql:c]-=d*ll
 cnt[c]+=d*(1-2*x)-d*d
 h[q]=x+d
```
Check signs: For right, cnt[c+1] -= d*rr, cnt[c-nr:c] += d*(1-rr_rev). For left, cnt[c+1] += d*(1-ll_rev), cnt[c-ql:c] -= d*ll. Good. `cnt[c] += d*(1-2*x)-d*d`; if d can negative, d*d positive, formula works? If d=-1 from x=1: -1*(1-2)-1 =1-1=0. If x=0, d=1:0. Good.
If x+d outside [0,1]? Feasible checks ensure within tolerance, but due FP maybe slight; apply clips h[q]? We can `h[q]=min(1,max(0,x+d))`. But c0 delta then inconsistency if clipped. Instead in pair ensure d <= bounds with small tolerance but no clip maybe. Use `np.clip` for safety:
```
new=x+d; new=min(1.0,max(0.0,new)); d=new-x
```
Recompute delta. Add.

`pair`:
```
def pair(h,n,dl,rad):
 h=project(h,n); best=h.copy(); cnt=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); bc=float(cnt.max()); acc=0
 dlist=(5e-4,2e-4,1e-4,5e-5,2e-5,1e-5)
 for d in dlist:
  for _ in range(3):
   if time.time()>=dl: break
   imp=False
   for i in range(n):
    if time.time()>=dl: break
    lo=max(0,i-rad); hi=min(n,i+rad+1)
    for j in range(lo,hi):
     if i==j: continue
     if h[i]<1.0 and h[j]>0.0:
      dm=min(d,1.0-h[i],h[j])
      if dm>1e-14:
       apply(i,dm,h,cnt,n); m=float(cnt.max())
       if m<bc-1e-12:
        bc=m; imp=True; acc+=1
       else:
        apply(j,dm,h,cnt,n); apply(i,-dm,h,cnt,n)
     if h[i]>0.0 and h[j]<1.0:
      dm=min(d,h[i],1.0-h[j])
      if dm>1e-14:
       apply(j,dm,h,cnt,n); m=float(cnt.max())
       if m<bc-1e-12:
        bc=m; imp=True; acc+=1
       else:
        apply(i,dm,h,cnt,n); apply(j,-dm,h,cnt,n)
    if acc and acc%2000==0:
      h2=project(h,n); c2=np.correlate(h2,1-h2,'full'); m2=float(c2.max())
      if m2<bc: bc=m2; best=h2.copy(); h=h2; cnt=c2
      else:
       h=best.copy(); cnt=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); bc=float(cnt.max())
      acc=0
   if not imp and _>0: break
  if not imp: break
 h=project(h,n); ch=c5(h,n); cb=c5(best,n)
 if ch<cb: best=h
 return best,min(ch,cb)
```
In second direction, move to j: `apply(j,dm)` then `apply(i,-dm)`. Revert: `apply(i,dm); apply(j,-dm)`. Good. But after first direction accepted, h changed; second direction uses new h. okay.
Potential bug: In inner loops, if first direction accepted, `bc` updated but `cnt` corresponds h. second direction if rejected reverts correctly. If first direction accepted and second also accepted, fine. If first direction accepted, then second direction rejected, h remains after first accepted. Fine.
`imp` break condition: `if not imp and _>0: break`? If first pass no improved, break; if improved, loop again. We can not break until no improved. The `for _ in range(3)` means max 3 passes. Remove `if _>0`. Use `if not imp: break` after pass. Good.
However if a pass has no improvement at start, break. If pass has improvements, next pass. Good.
The recompute block inside i loop if acc%2000 checks every candidate? It is after each pair? We placed after j loop? In code above after j within i? It should be after j loop maybe. In draft I put inside i after j but before loop end; it will execute every i if acc condition true? Need control. Better not recompute inside candidate loops frequently; use separate after each i if acc. But okay if condition every i. To avoid many project calls, check at top of i loop:
```
if acc%2000==0 and acc>0: recompute
```
at start of each i. We'll restructure compact:
for i in range(n):
 if acc and acc%2000==0: recompute; acc=0
 ... j loop
```
Good.
But if recompute resets h to best, current i index invalid? The loop continues with same i, okay.
Use `if not imp: break` after for i.

`golden0`:
```
def golden(n):
 h=np.zeros(n); k=n//2; ph=(1+5**.5)/2; p=(np.arange(k)*ph).astype(int)%n; h[p]=1; return project(h,n)
```

def thresh(h,n):
 h2=np.zeros(n); idx=np.argpartition(h, int(n/2))[int(n/2):]; h2[idx]=1; return project(h2,n)
```
If n even, top n/2. `argpartition` with kth = n/2; returns partition where elements before <=, after >=. Taking [n/2:] gives top n/2. Works. If duplicates okay.
`upscale`:

def up(h,n,m):
 xo=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False); xm=np.linspace(0,2,m,endpoint=False); return project(np.interp(xm,xo,h),m)
```

Implement `add_var_delta(q, delta, h, arr, n)`:
```
def add_var(q,delta,h,arr,n):
 c=n-1; x=h[q]; nr=n-1-q
 if nr>0:
  rr=h[q+1:q+n]
  arr[c+1:c+1+nr] -= delta*rr
  arr[c-nr:c] += delta*(1-rr[::-1])
 ql=q
 if ql>0:
  ll=h[q-ql:q]
  arr[c+1:c+1+ql] += delta*(1-ll[::-1])
  arr[c-ql:c] -= delta*ll
 arr[c] += delta*(1-2*x)-delta*delta
```
This uses original h; do not modify h.

 if m<bc:
   bc=m; h[i]+=dm; h[j]-=dm; cnt=cand; imp=True
# opposite similarly cand, h[j]+=dm; h[i]-=dm
```

```
def pair(h,n,dl,rad):
 h=project(h,n); best=h.copy(); cnt=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); tmp=np.empty_like(cnt); bc=float(cnt.max())
 for d in (5e-4,2e-4,1e-4,5e-5,2e-5,1e-5):
  imp=False
  for _ in range(3):
   if time.time()>=dl: break
   pass_imp=False
   for i in range(n):
    if time.time()>=dl: break
    lo=max(0,i-rad); hi=min(n,i+rad+1)
    for j in range(lo,hi):
     if i==j: continue
     ei=1.0-h[i]; ej=h[j]
     dm=min(d,ei,ej)
     if dm>1e-14:
      tmp[:]=cnt
      add_var(i,dm,h,tmp,n)
      add_var(j,-dm,h,tmp,n)
      m=float(tmp.max())
      if m<bc-1e-12:
       bc=m; h[i]+=dm; h[j]-=dm; cnt,tmp=tmp,cnt; pass_imp=True; imp=True
     dm=min(d,h[i],1.0-h[j])
     if dm>1e-14:
      tmp[:]=cnt
      add_var(j,dm,h,tmp,n)
      add_var(i,-dm,h,tmp,n)
      m=float(tmp.max())
      if m<bc-1e-12:
       bc=m; h[j]+=dm; h[i]-=dm; cnt,tmp=tmp,cnt; pass_imp=True; imp=True
   if not pass_imp: break
  # after d
  h2=project(h,n); c2=np.correlate(h2,1-h2,'full'); m2=float(c2.max())
  if m2 < bc: bc=m2; best=h2.copy()
  if m2 > bc + 1e-7:
    h=best.copy(); cnt=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
  else:
    h=h2
  cnt=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); tmp=np.empty_like(cnt); bc=float(cnt.max())??? Wait if m2<bc, bc should be min; if m2>bc+1e-7 reset bc to best max. Need be careful:
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
 t0=time.time(); dl=t0+budget_s-10
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 try: h0=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n=int(len(h0))
 except Exception: h0=golden(500); n=500
 bh=project(h0,n); bc=c5(bh,n)
 if dl - t0 > 150:
   end=t0+min(420, int(0.45*(dl-t0)))
   bh,bc=lbfgs(bh,n,end,MAIN)
 if dl - t0 > 250:
   end=time.time()+min(80, int(0.08*(dl-time.time())))
   bh,bc=pair(bh,n,end,15 if n<=700 else 10)
...
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

MAIN = ((100.0,1.0,200),(1000.0,5.0,250),(10000.0,50.0,300),(100000.0,200.0,350),(1000000.0,2000.0,500))
REST = ((10000.0,50.0,200),(500000.0,2000.0,300))
HR   = ((10000.0,50.0,250),(500000.0,2000.0,400))

def project(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if h.size < n:
        h = np.pad(h, (0, n - h.size))
    elif h.size > n:
        h = h[:n]
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    t = 0.5 * n
    lo = float(h.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(h.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum() > t:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(h - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(30):
        d = t - h.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-13:
            break
        if d > 0:
            r = 1.0 - h
            s = r.sum()
            if s < 1e-13:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (d / s) * r, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s < 1e-13:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (d / s) * h, 0.0, 1.0)
    d = t - h.sum()
    if abs(d) > 1e-15:
        f = np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
        if f.size:
            h[f] += d / f.size
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h

def c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')) * (2.0 / float(n)))

def fend(n):
    m = 2 * n - 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def smax(cnt, n, alpha):
    c = cnt * (2.0 / n)
    m = float(c.max())
    if alpha <= 0:
        return m
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    z -= z.max()
    return float(m + (z.max() + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z - z.max())))) / alpha)

def sw(cnt, n, alpha):
    c = cnt * (2.0 / n)
    m = c.max()
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    z -= z.max()
    w = np.exp(z)
    w /= w.sum()
    return w * (2.0 / n)

def gradw(W, h, n):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(n)
    c = n - 1
    wp = W[c + 1:c + n]
    wm = W[c - 1::-1]
    s = wp + wm
    L = fend(n)
    K = np.zeros(L)
    K[c + 1:c + n] = s
    K[c - 1::-1] = s
    T = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(h, n=L) * np.fft.rfft(K, n=L), n=L)[c:c + n]
    pp = np.zeros(n)
    pm = np.zeros(n)
    if s.size:
        pp[1:] = np.cumsum(wp)
        pm[1:] = np.cumsum(wm)
    return pm[n - 1::-1] + pp + W[c] - T - 2.0 * W[c] * h

def objp(x, n, alpha, mu):
    cnt = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, 'full')
    v = smax(cnt, n, alpha)
    s = x.sum() - 0.5 * n
    return v + 0.5 * mu * s * s

def gradp(x, n, alpha, mu):
    cnt = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, 'full')
    g = gradw(sw(cnt, n, alpha), x, n)
    g += mu * (x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
    return g

def objs(x, n, alpha):
    return smax(np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, 'full'), n, alpha)

def gsmooth(x, n, alpha):
    return gradw(sw(np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, 'full'), n, alpha), x, n)

def slqeq(x, n):
    return x.sum() - 0.5 * n

def slqjac(x, n):
    return np.ones_like(x)

def lbfgs(h, n, dl, cfgs):
    best = project(h, n)
    bc = c5(best, n)
    b = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    for alpha, mu, mx in cfgs:
        rem = dl - time.time()
        if rem < 12:
            break
        it = int(min(mx, max(10, int(rem * 3))))
        try:
            r = minimize(objp, best, gradp, args=(n, alpha, mu), method='L-BFGS-B',
                         bounds=b, options={'maxiter': it, 'ftol': 1e-16, 'gtol': 1e-12, 'maxls': 50})
            if hasattr(r, 'x'):
                h2 = project(r.x, n)
                c2 = c5(h2, n)
                if c2 < bc:
                    bc = c2
                    best = h2.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best, bc

def slq(h, n, dl, alpha, mx):
    h = project(h, n)
    bc = c5(h, n)
    rem = dl - time.time()
    if rem < 25:
        return h, bc
    it = int(min(mx, max(20, int(rem * 3))))
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': slqeq, 'jac': slqjac, 'args': (n,)}]
    try:
        r = minimize(objs, h, gsmooth, args=(n, alpha), method='SLSQP',
                     bounds=[(0.0, 1.0)] * n, constraints=cons,
                     options={'maxiter': it, 'ftol': 1e-15})
        if hasattr(r, 'x'):
            h2 = project(r.x, n)
            c2 = c5(h2, n)
            if c2 < bc:
                return h2.copy(), c2
    except Exception:
        pass
    return h, bc

def add_var(q, d, h, arr, n):
    c = n - 1
    x = h[q]
    nr = n - 1 - q
    if nr > 0:
        rr = h[q + 1:q + n]
        arr[c + 1:c + 1 + nr] -= d * rr
        arr[c - nr:c] += d * (1.0 - rr[::-1])
    ql = q
    if ql > 0:
        ll = h[q - ql:q]
        arr[c + 1:c + 1 + ql] += d * (1.0 - ll[::-1])
        arr[c - ql:c] -= d * ll
    arr[c] += d * (1.0 - 2.0 * x) - d * d

def pair(h, n, dl, rad):
    h = project(h, n)
    best = h.copy()
    cnt = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')
    tmp = np.empty_like(cnt)
    bc = float(cnt.max())
    cur = bc
    if n > 800:
        dlist = (1e-3, 5e-4, 2e-4, 1e-4)
    else:
        dlist = (5e-4, 2e-4, 1e-4, 5e-5, 2e-5)
    for d in dlist:
        imp = False
        for _ in range(3):
            if time.time() >= dl:
                break
            pi = False
            for i in range(n):
                if time.time() >= dl:
                    break
                lo = max(0, i - rad)
                hi = min(n, i + rad + 1)
                for j in range(lo, hi):
                    if i == j:
                        continue
                    dm = min(d, 1.0 - h[i], h[j])
                    if dm > 1e-14:
                        tmp[:]=cnt
                        add_var(i, dm, h, tmp, n)
                        add_var(j, -dm, h, tmp, n)
                        m = float(tmp.max())
                        if m < cur - 1e-12:
                            if m < bc:
                                bc = m
                                best = h.copy()
                            cur = m
                            h[i] += dm
                            h[j] -= dm
                            cnt, tmp = tmp, cnt
                            pi = True
                            imp = True
                    dm = min(d, h[i], 1.0 - h[j])
                    if dm > 1e-14:
                        tmp[:]=cnt
                        add_var(j, dm, h, tmp, n)
                        add_var(i, -dm, h, tmp, n)
                        m = float(tmp.max())
                        if m < cur - 1e-12:
                            if m < bc:
                                bc = m
                                best = h.copy()
                            cur = m
                            h[j] += dm
                            h[i] -= dm
                            cnt, tmp = tmp, cnt
                            pi = True
                            imp = True
            if not pi:
                break
        h2 = project(h, n)
        c2 = np.correlate(h2, 1.0 - h2, 'full')
        m2 = float(c2.max())
        if m2 < bc:
            bc = m2
            best = h2.copy()
        if m2 > bc + 1e-7:
            h = best.copy()
            c2 = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')
        else:
            h = h2
        cnt = c2
        tmp = np.empty_like(cnt)
        cur = float(cnt.max())
    h = project(h, n)
    ch = c5(h, n)
    best = project(best, n)
    cb = c5(best, n)
    if ch < cb:
        return h, ch
    return best, cb

def golden(n):
    h = np.zeros(n)
    k = n // 2
    ph = (1.0 + 5.0 ** 0.5) / 2.0
    p = (np.arange(k) * ph).astype(int) % n
    h[p] = 1.0
    return project(h, n)

def thresh(h, n):
    h2 = np.zeros(n)
    k = int(n / 2)
    idx = np.argpartition(h, k)[k:]
    h2[idx] = 1.0
    return project(h2, n)

def up(h, n, m):
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    xm = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, m, endpoint=False)
    return project(np.interp(xm, xo, h), m)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    dl = t0 + budget_s - 10
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = int(len(h0))
        if n < 2:
            raise ValueError
    except Exception:
        h0 = golden(500)
        n = 500
    bh = project(h0, n)
    bc = c5(bh, n)

    rem = dl - time.time()
    if rem > 180:
        end = time.time() + min(380, 0.42 * rem)
        bh, bc = lbfgs(bh, n, end, MAIN)

    rem = dl - time.time()
    if rem > 140:
        end = time.time() + min(80, 0.08 * rem)
        bh, bc = pair(bh, n, end, 15 if n <= 700 else 10)

    rem = dl - time.time()
    if rem > 170:
        end = time.time() + min(130, 0.16 * rem)
        h, c = slq(bh, n, end, 200000.0, 500)
        if c < bc:
            bh, bc = h, c

    rem = dl - time.time()
    if rem > 110:
        end = time.time() + min(55, 0.07 * rem)
        bh, bc = pair(bh, n, end, 10)

    rem = dl - time.time()
    if rem > 150:
        end = time.time() + min(130, 0.18 * rem)
        h, c = slq(bh, n, end, 2000000.0, 700)
        if c < bc:
            bh, bc = h, c

    rem = dl - time.time()
    if rem > 80:
        end = time.time() + min(50, 0.08 * rem)
        bh, bc = pair(bh, n, end, 8)

    rem = dl - time.time()
    if rem > 320 and n < 900:
        m = min(900, 2 * n)
        if m % 2:
            m -= 1
        if m <= n:
            m = min(900, n + 100)
        hu = up(bh, n, m)
        end = time.time() + min(200, 0.32 * rem)
        h, c = lbfgs(hu, m, end, HR)
        h2, c2 = h, c
        rem2 = dl - time.time()
        if c2 < bc and rem2 > 70:
            h2, c2 = slq(h2, m, min(dl - 30, time.time() + 0.25 * rem2), 1000000.0, 500)
        if c2 < bc:
            bh, bc, n = h2, c2, m

    rem = dl - time.time()
    if rem > 120:
        hp = project(bh + 0.08 * rng.randn(n), n)
        h, c = lbfgs(hp, n, min(dl - 30, time.time() + min(70, 0.2 * rem)), REST)
        if c < bc:
            bh, bc = h, c

    rem = dl - time.time()
    if rem > 90:
        hb = thresh(bh, n)
        h, c = lbfgs(hb, n, min(dl - 20, time.time() + min(50, 0.18 * rem)), REST)
        if c < bc:
            bh, bc = h, c

    rem = dl - time.time()
    if rem > 25:
        bh, bc = pair(bh, n, min(dl - 4, time.time() + min(20, 0.8 * rem)), 6)

    bh = project(bh, n)
    bc = c5(bh, n)
    return bh, bc, int(n)
```
